Projection simplex:
def project_simplex(v):
    # v array, project to sum1 nonneg.
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1, len(v)+1) > (cssv - 1))[0][-1]
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1) / (rho + 1)
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0)
Need v finite; if all -inf, return uniform. Since v=p - step*D positive maybe.

But p - stepD can have large negative; projection okay.

```
def compute_direction_fft(p, r):
    n=len(p); L=len(r)
    rf = r[::-1]
    nfft = 1 << (n+L-2).bit_length()  # length n+L-1? next pow2
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, nfft)
    Fr = np.fft.rfft(rf, nfft)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fr, nfft)[:n+L-1]
    if n==1: return conv[:1]
    return conv[2*n-2 : n-2 : -1]
```
Check length: n=4 L=7, n+L-2=9, bit_length? 9.bit_length=4, 1<<4=16; okay. If n+L-2 is power of two? Need nfft >= n+L-1. If m=n+L-1, nfft=1 << (m-1).bit_length(). Use m = n+L-1. Good.
For q: m=2*n-1, nfft=1 << (m-1).bit_length().

`gradient_polish` details:
```
def gradient_polish(p0, deadline, tag=""):
    n=len(p0); p=clip; p/=sum; logp=np.log(p)
    eps=1e-15
    q, qmax=fft_conv_max(p); score=2*n*qmax
    best=p.copy(); best_score=score
    beta = max(1.0, 5.0*n)
    beta_target = max(beta, 30.0*n)
    eta = 0.5
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed? but helper no seed? Use global rng? Pass rng)
```

       # maybe limit step norm? step = sign? 
       newlogp=logp - step
       # center to avoid drift
       newlogp -= np.mean(newlogp)

       p_new=softmax_vector(newlogp)
       s_new=score_fft(p_new)
       # acceptance: if s_new < score or anneal
       accept = s_new < score
       if not accept:
           temp = 2e-5 + 1e-5*n? Actually score units ~1-2, want temp maybe 0.01? But qmax difference small. If use score, temp=1e-3? Let's use qmax units: q_diff = (s_new-score)/(2n). temp_q = 1e-6? Hard. Use score temp=0.001 maybe. Accept worsening up to 0.001. Since target difference small.
       if accept:
           logp=newlogp; p=p_new; score=s_new; eta=min(1.2, eta*1.03); reject=0
       else:
           # try half step

       it+=1
    return best,best_score
```

def make_starts(n, rng, parent):
  starts=[]
  def add(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=float); p=np.clip(p,0,None)
    s=p.sum(); if s>0 and finite:
      p=p/s; starts.append(p.copy())
  x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  add(np.ones(n))
  for lam in (0.1,0.3,0.5,0.8,1.2,1.8,2.5,4,6):
     add(np.exp(-lam*x)); add(np.exp(lam*x))  # reverse
  for a in (-1.5,-0.7,0,0.7,1.5):
     for b in (-1,0,1):
        add(np.exp(a*x + b*x*x))
        add(np.exp(a*(1-x)+b*(1-x)*(1-x)))
  for freq in (1,2,3,5):
     for amp in (0.3,0.6,0.9):
       for phase in (0,np.pi/2,np.pi):
          v=1+amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*x/n + phase)
          add(np.clip(v,1e-6,None))
  for m in (8,16):
     for rep in (0,1,2):

       cp=np.exp(z); cp=(cp-cp.min()+1)
       add(np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,n), np.linspace(0,1,m), cp))

      add(1+2*np.exp(-((x-c)**2)/(2*w*w)))

  for freq in (2,4,8,16):

  # parent
  if parent is not None:
     arr=np.asarray(parent); ... normalize; add(arr_interp)
     add(arr[::-1] interp)
  # Deduplicate approximately? Not necessary but can limit.
  # Shuffle, return first max_starts maybe 16

```
def local_polish(p, deadline, rng, tag):
  best=p.copy(); best_score=score_fft(p)
  n=len(p)
  base_sigma=0.7
  while now<deadline-1:
    p2=p.copy()
    # random block multiplicative
    width=rng.integers(max(2,n//20), max(3,n//8))
    st=rng.integers(0,n-width)
    p2[st:st+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.2,size=width))
    # sometimes random point mass move
    if rng.random()<0.3:
       i=rng.integers(0,n); j=rng.integers(0,n); d=p[i]*rng.uniform(0.01,0.1)
       p2[i]-=d; p2[j]+=d
    normalize; s=score_fft(p2)
    if s < best_score:
       best=p2; best_score=s; print
    # occasional huge jump if no improve for long: p = (1-0.5)*p +0.5*random smooth
  return best,best_score
```

def seq_from_coeffs(coeffs, n):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   v=1.0
   for k,a,b in enumerate(coeffs[1:], start=1): # cos(kπx), sin(kπx)?

def _normalize_positive(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1000.0)
    v = v[np.isfinite(v)]? If size changes? Better if any nonfinite return uniform. But helper expected.

def _normalize_positive(v):
  v=np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64), nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
  v=np.clip(v,0.0,1e300)

`fft_convolution_linear(a, b=None)`: Maybe separate.
```
def _fft_linear_convolution(a,b):
  n=len(a); m=len(b) if b is not None else n
  full=n+m-1
  nfft=1 << (full-1).bit_length()
  A=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft)
  B=np.fft.rfft(b,nfft) if b is not None else A
  out=np.fft.irfft(A*B,nfft)[:full]
  return out
```
For square conv q:
```
def _conv_square(p):

  out=np.where(out>0, out, 0.0)

  return out

Direction:
```
def _direction_for_weights(p, r):
  n=len(p); L=len(r)
  if n==1: return np.array([float(r[0])])
  rf=r[::-1]
  full=n+L-1
  nfft=1 << (full-1).bit_length()
  Fp=np.fft.rfft(p, nfft)
  Fr=np.fft.rfft(rf, nfft)
  conv=np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fr, nfft)[:full]
  idx=2*n-2
  D=conv[idx : n-2 : -1]
  # ensure length n; if not pad
  if len(D)!=n: D = D[:n] ...
  return np.maximum(D,0)
```
Let's verify slice for n=1 handled. For n=2: L=3, full4. indices D0=conv[2], D1=conv[1]? slice start2 stop0 step -1 -> [2,1] length2 good. For n=1: stop -1? okay but handle.

Check direction formula with q derivative: For p=[x,y], r=(0,1,0), direction D_i should be p_{k-i} where k=1: D0=p1, D1=p0? q1=2xy derivative 2y,2x; D = p_j? C_i=∑ r_{i+j}p_j: i=0 j=1 r1 p1 = y; i=1 j=0 r1 p0=x. Good.

def _softmax_weights(q, beta):
  qmax=q.max()
  if beta<=0: return np.full(len(q),1/len(q))
  z=beta*(q-qmax)
  # if min z < -745, underflow okay. But if all z very negative? max 0.
  w=np.exp(z)
  s=w.sum(); if s<=0: w=np.zeros; w[argmax]=1
  return w/s
```

`resample`:
```
def _resample_sequence(seq,n):
   arr=np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(seq,dtype=np.float64))
   if arr.ndim !=1 or arr.size==0: return None
   arr=np.clip(arr,0,1e300)
   s=arr.sum(); if s<=0: return None
   arr=arr/s
   if arr.size==n: return arr
   x0=np.linspace(0,1,arr.size)
   x1=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   out=np.interp(x1,x0,arr)
   return _normalize_positive(out)
```

`make_starts(n, rng, parent)`:

def _push_start(starts, arr):
    p=_normalize_positive(arr)
    if p is not None and p.size>0:
       # ensure not too similar? just append
       starts.append(p)
```
Then make_starts calls.
```
def _make_starts(n, rng, parent):
   starts=[]
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   _push_start(starts, np.ones(n))
   for lam in (0.1,0.3,0.5,0.8,1.2,1.8,2.5,4.0,6.0):
      _push_start(starts, np.exp(-lam*x))
      _push_start(starts, np.exp(lam*x))
   for a in (-2.0,-1.0,-0.5,0.0,0.5,1.0,2.0):
      for b in (-2.0,-1.0,0.0,1.0,2.0):
         _push_start(starts, np.exp(a*x + b*x*x))
   for freq in (1,2,3,5,8):
      for amp in (0.3,0.6,0.9):
         for phase in (0.0, 0.785398, 1.570796, 3.141593):
            v=1.0 + amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*x/ max(1,n-1) + phase)
            _push_start(starts, np.maximum(v,1e-6))
   for m in (8,16,32):
      for rep in range(3):
         z=rng.normal(0.0,0.8,m)
         z=z - np.mean(z)
         v=np.exp(np.clip(z, -3,3))
         v=v - v.min() + 1.0
         xs=np.linspace(0,1,m)
         _push_start(starts, np.interp(x,xs,v))
   # gauss bumps
   for c in (0.2,0.5,0.8):
      for w in (0.05,0.12,0.25):
         _push_start(starts, 1.0+2.0*np.exp(-((x-c)**2)/(2*w*w)))
   # parent
   if parent is not None:
      p=_resample_sequence(parent,n)
      if p is not None:
         _push_start(starts,p)
         _push_start(starts,p[::-1])
   # Evaluate scores and keep top max_starts
   candidates=[]
   for p in starts:
      try: sc=score_fft(p)
      except: sc=np.inf
      if np.isfinite(sc): candidates.append((sc,p))
   candidates.sort(key=lambda t:t[0])  # lambda? Rule no lambda functions! Need avoid.
```

def _gradient_polish(p0, deadline, rng, beta_start, beta_end, tag):
   start_time=time.monotonic()
   p=_normalize_positive(p0).copy()
   n=len(p)
   logp=np.log(p)
   q=qconv; qmax=q.max(); score=2*n*qmax
   best=p.copy(); best_score=score
   total_time=max(1.0, deadline-start_time)
   beta=beta_start
   eta=0.5
   reject_streak=0
   no_improve_count=0
   improved_at=score
   temp=0.02
   it=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.05:
      if it % 2000 == 0: print(...)
      elapsed=time.monotonic()-start_time
      frac=min(1.0, elapsed/total_time)
      beta=beta_start+(beta_end-beta_start)*frac
      # if no improve for many, reheat?
      if no_improve_count > 8000 or reject_streak > 100:
          logp = logp + rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, n)
          logp -= np.mean(logp)
          p=_softmax_from_log(logp)
          q=...; score=...
          no_improve_count=0; reject_streak=0; eta=0.4; beta=max(beta_start,beta*0.5)
      w=_softmax_weights(q,beta)
      D=_direction_for_weights(p,w)
      mean=np.dot(p,D)
      Dc=D-mean
      scale=np.max(np.abs(Dc))
      if not finite or scale < 1e-14:
         logp = logp + rng.normal(0,0.05,n); p=...; q...; continue
      for attempt in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
         step=eta*attempt*(Dc/scale)
         # maybe cap step
         step=np.clip(step, -2.0, 2.0)
         newlogp=logp - step
         newlogp -= np.mean(newlogp)
         p_new=_softmax_from_log(newlogp)
         q_new=_conv_square(p_new)
         qmax_new=q_new.max(); s_new=2*n*qmax_new
         if s_new < score or (s_new-score)/temp < -np.log(rng.random()+1e-15)? Actually accept if s_new<score or random < exp(-(s_new-score)/temp)
             accept=True; break
      if not accept:
         reject_streak+=1; eta=max(0.05, eta*0.8)
         # keep q current
         continue
      logp=newlogp; p=p_new; q=q_new; qmax=qmax_new; score=s_new
      reject_streak=0; eta=min(1.2, eta*1.05)
      if score < best_score:
          best=p.copy(); best_score=score; no_improve_count=0; temp=max(0.001,temp*0.9)
          print(tag, score)
      else:
          no_improve_count+=1
      temp=max(0.0005, temp*0.9995)
      it+=1
   return best,best_score
```

def _local_polish(p0, deadline, rng, tag):
    p=_normalize_positive(p0).copy(); n=len(p); best=p.copy(); best_score=score_fft(p)
    sigma=0.35
    no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline-0.1:
       p2=p.copy()
       # random block
       maxw=max(2, n//16); minw=2
       w=int(rng.integers(minw, maxw+1))
       st=int(rng.integers(0, n-w+1))
       factors=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, w))
       p2[st:st+w]*=factors
       # maybe random mirror
       if rng.random() < 0.1: p2 = 0.9*p +0.1*p2
       p2=_normalize_positive(p2)
       s2=score_fft(p2)
       if s2 < best_score*(1-1e-12):
          p=p2; best=p.copy(); best_score=s2; no_improve=0; sigma=max(0.05, sigma*0.98); print
       else:
          no_improve+=1
          if no_improve > 2000:
             # reset to best with noise
             p=best.copy(); p += p*rng.normal(0,0.2,n)? normalize; best_score? no
             no_improve=0; sigma=0.5
    return best,best_score
```

def _fallback_sequence(n=512):

  n=len(p); if nfft None compute

  q=np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fp,nfft)[:2*n-1]

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from operator import itemgetter


"""
Step-function coefficient search.

The objective is scale invariant.  We normalize candidates to sum 1 and
minimize the maximum full linear convolution coefficient.  The search combines:

  1. several analytic / random low-level starts (exponential ramps, splines,
     periodic carriers, sparse masks, parent resampling);
  2. a log-space gradient/annealed annealer using FFT convolutions;
  3. multi-resolution polishing (512 -> 1024, optional 2048);
  4. a final random local walk.

The returned sequence is scaled to remain safely inside the evaluator's checks.
"""


def _nfft(length):
    if length <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (length - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize_positive(v):
    if v is None:
        return None
    arr = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.ndim != 1:
        arr = arr.reshape(-1)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1e6)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return arr / s


def _softmax_from_log(logp):
    n = len(logp)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    logp = np.asarray(logp, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(logp)):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(logp))
    z = logp - m
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
    v = np.exp(z)
    s = float(v.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return v / s


def _fft_square(p):
    n = len(p)
    full = 2 * n - 1
    nfft = _nfft(full)
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, nfft)
    q = np.fft.irfft(F * F, nfft)[:full]
    q = np.where(q > 0.0, q, 0.0)
    return q


def _score_fft(p):
    if p is None:
        return np.inf
    q = _fft_square(p)
    if q.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    m = float(np.max(q))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * len(p) * m)


def _sq_fft_with_nfft(p, nfft):
    n = len(p)
    full = 2 * n - 1
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, nfft)
    q = np.fft.irfft(Fp * Fp, nfft)[:full]
    q = np.where(q > 0.0, q, 0.0)
    return Fp, q


def _direction_with_fp(p, Fp, r, nfft):
    n = len(p)
    L = len(r)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(r[0]) if r.size else 0.0], dtype=np.float64)

    rf = r[::-1]
    Fr = np.fft.rfft(rf, nfft)
    full = n + L - 1
    conv = np.fft.irfft(Fp * Fr, nfft)[:full]
    # D_i = sum_j p_j * r_{i+j}
    D = conv[2 * n - 2 : n - 2 : -1]
    if D.size != n:
        tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(n, D.size)
        tmp[:m] = D[:m]
        D = tmp
    D = np.where(D > 0.0, D, 0.0)
    return D


def _softmax_weights(q, beta):
    if beta <= 0.0:
        return np.full(len(q), 1.0 / len(q), dtype=np.float64)
    qmax = float(np.max(q))
    z = beta * (q - qmax)
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
    w = np.exp(z)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(len(q), dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
        return w
    return w / s


def _resample_sequence(seq, n):
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.ndim != 1:
        arr = arr.reshape(-1)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1e6)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    arr = arr / s
    if arr.size == n:
        return arr
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, arr.size)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(x1, x0, arr)
    return _normalize_positive(out)


def _push_start(starts, arr):
    p = _normalize_positive(arr)
    if p is not None and np.isfinite(p).all():
        starts.append(p.copy())


def _make_starts(n, rng, parent=None, max_starts=8):
    starts = []
    if n <= 0:
        return []
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    _push_start(starts, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    for lam in (0.05, 0.15, 0.3, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0, 1.2, 1.6, 2.0, 2.8, 4.0, 6.0):
        _push_start(starts, np.exp(-lam * x))
        _push_start(starts, np.exp(lam * x))

    for a in (-2.0, -1.2, -0.7, -0.3, 0.0, 0.3, 0.7, 1.2, 2.0):
        for b in (-2.0, -1.0, -0.2, 0.0, 0.2, 1.0, 2.0):
            _push_start(starts, np.exp(a * x + b * x * x))
            _push_start(starts, np.exp(a * (1.0 - x) + b * (1.0 - x) * (1.0 - x)))

    for c in (0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.5):
        for aa in (0.5, 0.8, 1.0, 1.4, 2.0):
            _push_start(starts, 1.0 / np.power(x + c, aa))
            _push_start(starts, 1.0 / np.power((1.0 - x) + c, aa))

    den = max(1, n - 1)
    for freq in (1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 8, 13):
        for amp in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 0.95):
            for phase in (0.0, 0.8, 1.6, 2.4, 3.141592653589793):
                v = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x / den + phase)
                _push_start(starts, np.maximum(v, 1e-6))
                v2 = 1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x / den + phase)
                _push_start(starts, np.maximum(v2, 1e-6))

    for m in (8, 12, 16, 24):
        for _rep in range(3):
            z = rng.normal(0.0, 0.8, size=m)
            z = z - float(np.mean(z))
            v = np.exp(np.clip(z, -3.0, 3.0))
            v = v - float(np.min(v)) + 1.0
            xs = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
            _push_start(starts, np.interp(x, xs, v))

    for center in (0.15, 0.35, 0.5, 0.65, 0.85):
        for w in (0.03, 0.07, 0.13, 0.25):
            _push_start(starts, 1.0 + 2.0 * np.exp(-(((x - center) ** 2) / (2.0 * w * w))))
            _push_start(starts, 2.0 - 1.5 * np.exp(-(((x - center) ** 2) / (2.0 * w * w))))

    for density in (0.25, 0.4, 0.5, 0.6, 0.75):
        for boost in (2.0, 4.0, 8.0, 16.0):
            mask = rng.random(n) < density
            v = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            v[mask] *= boost
            _push_start(starts, v)
            mask2 = rng.random(n) < density
            v2 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            v2[mask2] *= boost
            _push_start(starts, v2)

    for _rep in range(6):
        v = 0.05 + rng.random(n)
        _push_start(starts, v)

    if parent is not None:
        p = _resample_sequence(parent, n)
        if p is not None:
            _push_start(starts, p)
            _push_start(starts, p[::-1])
            pm = 0.5 * p + 0.5 * p[::-1]
            _push_start(starts, pm)

    scored = []
    for p in starts:
        try:
            s = _score_fft(p)
        except Exception:
            s = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(s):
            scored.append((float(s), p))

    if not scored:
        u = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        return [u]

    scored.sort(key=itemgetter(0))
    if max_starts <= 0:
        max_starts = 8
    return [p for _s, p in scored[:max_starts]]


def _gradient_polish(p0, deadline, rng, beta_start, beta_end, tag=""):
    p = _normalize_positive(p0)
    if p is None:
        return None, np.inf
    n = len(p)
    if n == 0:
        return None, np.inf

    if float(np.max(p) - np.min(p)) < 1e-12:
        p = p + 1e-3 * (rng.random(n) - 0.5)
        p = _normalize_positive(p)
        if p is None:
            return _normalize_positive(np.ones(n)), _score_fft(_normalize_positive(np.ones(n)))

    p = p.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    logp = np.log(p)
    nfft = _nfft(3 * n - 2)
    Fp, q = _sq_fft_with_nfft(p, nfft)
    qmax = float(np.max(q))
    score = float(2.0 * n * qmax)

    best = p.copy()
    best_score = score
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    total_time = max(1.0, float(deadline - start_time))

    eta = 0.45
    reject_streak = 0
    no_improve_count = 0
    last_improve_time = start_time
    temp = 0.035

    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.05:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if it == 0:
            print(f"[{tag}] start score={score:.9f} n={n}", flush=True)
        if it > 2000 and (now - last_improve_time) > 120.0:
            break

        frac = min(1.0, max(0.0, (now - start_time) / total_time))
        beta = beta_start + (beta_end - beta_start) * frac

        if score > best_score * 1.05 + 1e-12:
            logp = np.log(best)
            p = best.copy()
            Fp, q = _sq_fft_with_nfft(p, nfft)
            score = float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(q)))
            reject_streak = 0
            no_improve_count = 0
            last_improve_time = now

        w = _softmax_weights(q, beta)
        D = _direction_with_fp(p, Fp, w, nfft)
        meanD = float(np.dot(p, D))
        Dc = D - meanD
        scaleD = float(np.max(np.abs(Dc)))

        if not np.isfinite(scaleD) or scaleD < 1e-14:
            logp = logp + 0.08 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
            logp -= float(np.mean(logp))
            p = _softmax_from_log(logp)
            Fp, q = _sq_fft_with_nfft(p, nfft)
            score = float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(q)))
            no_improve_count += 1
            reject_streak = 0
            continue

        step = eta * (Dc / scaleD)
        step = np.clip(step, -1.5, 1.5)
        newlogp = logp - step
        newlogp -= float(np.mean(newlogp))
        p_new = _softmax_from_log(newlogp)

        try:
            Fp_new, q_new = _sq_fft_with_nfft(p_new, nfft)
            qmax_new = float(np.max(q_new))
            s_new = float(2.0 * n * qmax_new)
        except Exception:
            s_new = np.inf
            Fp_new = None
            q_new = None
            p_new = None

        accept = False
        if np.isfinite(s_new):
            if s_new < score:
                accept = True
            else:
                delta = s_new - score
                if delta < temp and rng.random() < float(np.exp(-delta / temp)):
                    accept = True

        if accept and p_new is not None:
            logp = newlogp
            p = p_new
            Fp = Fp_new
            q = q_new
            qmax = qmax_new
            score = s_new
            reject_streak = 0
            eta = min(1.1, eta * 1.04)
            if score < best_score:
                best = p.copy()
                best_score = score
                no_improve_count = 0
                last_improve_time = time.monotonic()
                temp = max(0.0008, temp * 0.97)
                if it % 5000 == 0:
                    print(f"[{tag}] improve it={it} score={score:.9f}", flush=True)
                else:
                    pass
            else:
                no_improve_count += 1
        else:
            reject_streak += 1
            no_improve_count += 1
            eta = max(0.05, eta * 0.65)
            if eta < 0.06 or reject_streak > 120:
                logp = np.log(best) + 0.06 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
                logp -= float(np.mean(logp))
                p = _softmax_from_log(logp)
                Fp, q = _sq_fft_with_nfft(p, nfft)
                score = float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(q)))
                eta = 0.35
                reject_streak = 0
                no_improve_count = 0

        it += 1

    if best_score > score and np.isfinite(score):
        best = p.copy()
        best_score = score

    print(f"[{tag}] polish done it={it} best={best_score:.9f}", flush=True)
    return best, best_score


def _local_polish(p0, deadline, rng, tag=""):
    p = _normalize_positive(p0)
    if p is None:
        return None, np.inf
    n = len(p)
    if n == 0:
        return None, np.inf

    best = p.copy()
    best_score = _score_fft(best)
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    last_improve = start_time
    sigma = 0.35
    no_improve = 0

    print(f"[{tag}] local start score={best_score:.9f}", flush=True)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.1:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if no_improve > 0 and (now - last_improve) > 120.0:
            break

        p2 = p.copy()
        min_w = max(2, n // 64)
        max_w = max(min_w + 1, n // 12)
        w = int(rng.integers(min_w, max_w + 1))
        st = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))
        p2[st:st + w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, w))

        if rng.random() < 0.25:
            i = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            j = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            amount = max(0.0, p[i] * rng.uniform(0.01, 0.15))
            p2[i] -= amount
            p2[j] += amount

        p2 = _normalize_positive(p2)
        if p2 is None:
            continue

        try:
            s2 = _score_fft(p2)
        except Exception:
            s2 = np.inf

        if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-12):
            best = p2
            best_score = s2
            p = p2
            no_improve = 0
            last_improve = time.monotonic()
            sigma = max(0.05, sigma * 0.985)
            if last_improve - start_time < 0:
                pass
            print(f"[{tag}] local improve score={s2:.9f}", flush=True)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 3000:
                p = best.copy()
                p = p * (1.0 + 0.15 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n))
                p = _normalize_positive(p)
                if p is None:
                    p = best.copy()
                best_score = _score_fft(p)
                sigma = 0.55
                no_improve = 0

    print(f"[{tag}] local done best={best_score:.9f}", flush=True)
    return best, best_score


def _scale_sequence_for_evaluator(p):
    p = _normalize_positive(p)
    if p is None:
        p = np.exp(-np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 512))
        p = _normalize_positive(p)
    maxv = float(np.max(p))
    if not np.isfinite(maxv) or maxv <= 0.0:
        scale = 10.0
    else:
        scale = 50.0 / maxv
        if maxv * scale > 1000.0:
            scale = 1000.0 / maxv
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s * scale < 0.01:
            scale = max(scale, 0.01 / s)
    seq = p * float(scale)
    seq = np.nan_to_num(seq, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    ss = float(seq.sum())
    if ss < 0.01:
        if ss > 0.0:
            seq = seq * (0.01 / ss)
        else:
            seq = np.full(np.size(seq), 0.01)
    return np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 2400.0

    start_all = time.monotonic()
    if budget_s < 60.0:
        p = np.exp(-np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 512))
        p = _normalize_positive(p)
        seq = _scale_sequence_for_evaluator(p)
        return [float(v) for v in seq]

    deadline_all = start_all + max(60.0, budget_s - 45.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed if seed is not None else 1234567)

    parent = None
    try:
        parent = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    best = None
    best_score = np.inf
    best_n = 0

    # Stage 1: moderate resolution, several starts.
    n1 = 512
    now = time.monotonic()
    stage1_len = min(420.0, max(60.0, (deadline_all - now) * 0.22))
    stage1_deadline = min(deadline_all, now + stage1_len)
    starts1 = _make_starts(n1, rng, parent, max_starts=7)

    idx = 0
    while idx < len(starts1) and time.monotonic() < stage1_deadline - 2.0:
        rem = stage1_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 5.0:
            break
        allowed = min(180.0, rem * 0.75)
        if allowed < 5.0:
            break
        cand_deadline = min(stage1_deadline, time.monotonic() + allowed)
        p, s = _gradient_polish(starts1[idx], cand_deadline, rng, 2.0 * n1, 30.0 * n1, tag=f"s1-{idx}")
        if p is not None and np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
            best = p
            best_score = float(s)
            best_n = len(p)
            print(f"[stage1] new global score={best_score:.9f}", flush=True)
        idx += 1

    # Stage 2: higher resolution, using current best + fresh starts.
    n2 = 1024
    now = time.monotonic()
    if deadline_all - now > 180.0:
        stage2_len = min(1600.0, max(180.0, (deadline_all - now) * 0.52))
        stage2_deadline = min(deadline_all, now + stage2_len)

        candidates = []
        if best is not None:
            rp = _resample_sequence(best, n2)
            if rp is not None:
                candidates.append(rp)
            rp2 = _resample_sequence(best[::-1], n2)
            if rp2 is not None:
                candidates.append(rp2)

        starts2 = _make_starts(n2, rng, parent, max_starts=7)
        candidates.extend(starts2)

        scored = []
        for cand in candidates:
            try:
                sc = _score_fft(cand)
            except Exception:
                sc = np.inf
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                scored.append((float(sc), cand))
        scored.sort(key=itemgetter(0))
        candidates = [c for _s, c in scored[:8]]

        idx = 0
        while idx < len(candidates) and time.monotonic() < stage2_deadline - 2.0:
            rem = stage2_deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 8.0:
                break
            allowed = min(360.0, rem * 0.65)
            if allowed < 8.0:
                break
            cand_deadline = min(stage2_deadline, time.monotonic() + allowed)
            p, s = _gradient_polish(candidates[idx], cand_deadline, rng, 2.0 * n2, 30.0 * n2, tag=f"s2-{idx}")
            if p is not None and np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
                best = p
                best_score = float(s)
                best_n = len(p)
                print(f"[stage2] new global score={best_score:.9f}", flush=True)
            idx += 1

    # Stage 3: final polish on current best.
    now = time.monotonic()
    if deadline_all - now > 60.0:
        final_n = best_n if best_n > 0 else 1024
        # Optional very-high-resolution attempt only if time and score suggest it.
        if (deadline_all - now) > 1000.0 and best_score > 1.56:
            final_n = 2048

        if best is None:
            pstart = _normalize_positive(np.exp(-np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, final_n)))
        else:
            pstart = _resample_sequence(best, final_n)
            if pstart is None:
                pstart = best.copy()

        remaining = deadline_all - time.monotonic()
        growal = min(min(900.0, remaining * 0.55), deadline_all - 30.0)
        if growal > 10.0:
            p, s = _gradient_polish(
                pstart,
                time.monotonic() + growal,
                rng,
                2.0 * final_n,
                35.0 * final_n,
                tag="final-gradient"
            )
            if p is not None and np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
                best = p
                best_score = float(s)
                best_n = len(p)
                print(f"[final-gradient] new global score={best_score:.9f}", flush=True)

        now = time.monotonic()
        if deadline_all - now > 20.0 and best is not None:
            plocal = _resample_sequence(best, best_n) if best_n != len(best) else best
            if plocal is None:
                plocal = best.copy()
            local_deadline = min(deadline_all, time.monotonic() + max(15.0, (deadline_all - now) * 0.9))
            p, s = _local_polish(plocal, local_deadline, rng, tag="final-local")
            if p is not None and np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
                best = p
                best_score = float(s)
                best_n = len(p)
                print(f"[final-local] new global score={best_score:.9f}", flush=True)

    if best is None:
        p = np.exp(-np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 512))
        best = _normalize_positive(p)
        best_n = 512

    seq = _scale_sequence_for_evaluator(best)
    seq_list = [float(v) for v in seq]

    try:
        exact = float(evaluate_sequence(seq_list))
        print(f"[final] exact score FFT estimate={best_score:.9f} exact={exact:.9f} len={len(seq_list)}", flush=True)
    except Exception as exc:
        print("[final] exact evaluator failed:", exc, flush=True)
        # Very conservative fallback, still valid and likely better than a broken return.
        fb = np.exp(-np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 512))
        fb = _normalize_positive(fb)
        seq = _scale_sequence_for_evaluator(fb)
        seq_list = [float(v) for v in seq]

    return seq_list
```
